      16: /*
      17:  * On macOS, DMA coherent memory is allocated by the kext using

      20:  * before probe so the fallback path never runs in production.
      21:  */
      22: struct rtw88_dma_alloc_ops {
      23:     void      *(*alloc_coherent)(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      24:                                   dma_addr_t *dma_handle, gfp_t flag);
      25:     void       (*free_coherent)(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      26:                                  void *cpu_addr, dma_addr_t dma_handle);

      39: static inline void *dma_alloc_coherent(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      40:                                         dma_addr_t *dma_handle, gfp_t flag)
      41: {
      42:     if (rtw88_dma_ops && rtw88_dma_ops->alloc_coherent)
      43:         return rtw88_dma_ops->alloc_coherent(dev, size, dma_handle, flag);
      44:     /* Fallback: plain kernel allocation (not DMA-safe) */
      45:     void *ptr = kzalloc(size, GFP_KERNEL);
      46:     if (ptr) *dma_handle = (dma_addr_t)(uintptr_t)ptr;
      47:     return ptr;
      48: }
      49: 
      50: static inline void dma_free_coherent(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      51:                                       void *cpu_addr, dma_addr_t dma_handle)
      52: {
      53:     if (rtw88_dma_ops && rtw88_dma_ops->free_coherent) {
      54:         rtw88_dma_ops->free_coherent(dev, size, cpu_addr, dma_handle);
      55:         return;
      56:     }
      57:     kfree(cpu_addr);
      58: }
      59: 
      60: static inline dma_addr_t dma_map_single(struct device *dev, void *ptr,
      61:                                           size_t size, int direction)
      62: {
      63:     if (rtw88_dma_ops && rtw88_dma_ops->map_single)
      64:         return rtw88_dma_ops->map_single(dev, ptr, size, direction);
      65:     return (dma_addr_t)(uintptr_t)ptr;
      66: }
      67: 
      68: static inline void dma_unmap_single(struct device *dev, dma_addr_t addr,
      69:                                      size_t size, int direction)
      70: {
      71:     if (rtw88_dma_ops && rtw88_dma_ops->unmap_single)
      72:         rtw88_dma_ops->unmap_single(dev, addr, size, direction);
      73: }
      74:

      76:                                              dma_addr_t addr,
